# Skills · L · part 22

813 entries, lit-scout to live-report. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lit-scout**: Literature discovery, triage, and synthesis for mRNA/LNP delivery, gene editing, and nanomedicine. Use to search and summarize papers, build reference lists, track a subfield, screen preprints, or pull compound/target data. Uses connected bio-research MCP tools (PubMed, bioRxiv, Consensus, ChEMBL, Open Targets, ClinicalTrials). Triggers on "find papers", "literature review", "what's new in", "summarize this paper", "citations for", "recent preprints". ([allenlee0430/allenlee-lab-skills](https://github.com/allenlee0430/allenlee-lab-skills/tree/HEAD/lit-scout))
- **lit-search**: Runs an exhaustive time-windowed literature search over a research topic and delivers a quality-tiered DOI list plus a matching formatted reference list, with measurable recall (gold-set recall, PRISMA counts, citation-closure saturation). Use for systematic reviews / SLR, the literature-retrieval half of 开题报告 (the writing half is the research-proposal skill, which consumes this skill's dois.md / references), 挑战赛调研, or any "把某方向近 N 年文献检索全/理一遍/梳理一遍/查全" request. Builds the corpus — not for reading or summarizing the papers themselves, and not for writing the review text. Not for casual "find me… ([luwill/research-skills](https://github.com/luwill/research-skills/tree/HEAD/lit-search) · ★843)
